Tamanho: 1.22KB
Modificado: 06/28/2025 14:36:52

   1  services:
   2    i9script-web:
   3      build:
   4        context: .
   5        dockerfile: Dockerfile
   6      container_name: i9script-website
   7      ports:
   8        - "4444:80"
   9      volumes:
  10        # Volume para desenvolvimento - permite hot reload
  11        - ./:/usr/share/nginx/html/:ro
  12        - ./nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf:ro
  13      environment:
  14        - NGINX_HOST=localhost
  15        - NGINX_PORT=80
  16        - TZ=America/Manaus
  17      restart: unless-stopped
  18      healthcheck:
  19        test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost/health"]
  20        interval: 30s
  21        timeout: 10s
  22        retries: 3
  23        start_period: 30s
  24      labels:
  25        - "com.i9script.description=i9Script Website Container"
  26        - "com.i9script.version=1.0"
  27  
  28    # ServiÃ§o opcional para logs e monitoramento
  29    nginx-logs:
  30      image: busybox:latest
  31      container_name: i9script-logs
  32      volumes:
  33        - logs-volume:/var/log/nginx
  34      command: sh -c "sleep 10 && tail -f /var/log/nginx/access.log /var/log/nginx/error.log 2>/dev/null || echo 'Aguardando logs...'"
  35      depends_on:
  36        - i9script-web
  37      restart: unless-stopped
  38      labels:
  39        - "com.i9script.description=i9Script Logs Monitor"
  40  
  41  volumes:
  42    logs-volume:
  43      name: i9script-logs
  44      driver: local

ARQUIVO: Dockerfile
Tamanho: 0.59KB
Modificado: 06/28/2025 14:29:17
══════════════════════════════════════════
   1  # Use nginx como servidor web
   2  FROM nginx:alpine
   3  
   4  # Instalar curl para health check
   5  RUN apk add --no-cache curl
   6  
   7  # Remover configuraÃ§Ã£o padrÃ£o do nginx
   8  RUN rm -rf /usr/share/nginx/html/*
   9  
  10  # Copiar arquivos do projeto para o diretÃ³rio do nginx
  11  COPY . /usr/share/nginx/html/
  12  
  13  # ConfiguraÃ§Ã£o customizada do nginx
  14  COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
  15  
  16  # Expor porta 80
  17  EXPOSE 80
  18  
  19  # Health check
  20  HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  21    CMD curl -f http://localhost/ || exit 1
  22  
  23  # Comando para iniciar o nginx
  24  CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]

2488              }

2494                  whatsappLink.href = `https://wa.me/5592984654008?text=${message}`;
2495              }
2496

2498              document.documentElement.lang = lang === 'pt' ? 'pt-BR' : 'en-US';
2499

2502          }

2580          // EmailJS Configuration

2582          // 1. Acesse https://www.emailjs.com/
2583          // 2. Crie uma conta gratuita
2584          // 3. Adicione um serviÃ§o de email (Gmail, Outlook, etc.)
2585          // 4. Crie um template de email
2586          // 5. Substitua as chaves abaixo pelas suas chaves reais
2587          const EMAILJS_CONFIG = {
2588              publicKey: 'YOUR_PUBLIC_KEY', // Substitua pela sua chave pÃºblica do EmailJS
2589              serviceId: 'YOUR_SERVICE_ID', // Substitua pelo seu Service ID
2590              templateId: 'YOUR_TEMPLATE_ID' // Substitua pelo seu Template ID
2591          };

2593          // TEMPLATE SUGERIDO PARA O EMAILJS:
2594          /*
2595          Assunto: [i9Script] Nova mensagem de {{from_name}}
2596          
2597          Corpo do email:
2598          ðŸš€ NOVA MENSAGEM DO SITE i9Script

2611          ---
2612          Para responder, use: {{reply_to}}

2615          // Initialize EmailJS
2616          function initEmailJS() {
2617              if (typeof emailjs !== 'undefined') {
2618                  try {
2619                      emailjs.init(EMAILJS_CONFIG.publicKey);
2620                      return true;
2621                  } catch (error) {
2622                      console.warn('EmailJS initialization failed:', error);
2623                      return false;
2624                  }
2625              }
2626              return false;
2627          }
2628  
2629          // Check if EmailJS is properly configured
2630          function isEmailJSConfigured() {
2631              return EMAILJS_CONFIG.publicKey !== 'YOUR_PUBLIC_KEY' && 
2632                     EMAILJS_CONFIG.serviceId !== 'YOUR_SERVICE_ID' && 
2633                     EMAILJS_CONFIG.templateId !== 'YOUR_TEMPLATE_ID';
2634          }
2635  
2636          // Send email via EmailJS
2637          async function sendEmailViaEmailJS(formData) {
2638              try {
2639                  if (!isEmailJSConfigured()) {
2640                      throw new Error('EmailJS not configured - using fallback');
2641                  }
2642  
2643                  if (!initEmailJS()) {
2644                      throw new Error('EmailJS not available');
2645                  }
2646  
2647                  const templateParams = {
2648                      from_name: formData.name,
2649                      from_email: formData.email,
2650                      project_type: formData.project,
2651                      message: formData.message,
2652                      to_email: 'contato@i9script.com',
2653                      reply_to: formData.email,
2654                      date: new Date().toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
2655                      user_agent: navigator.userAgent.substring(0, 100)
2656                  };
2657  
2658                  const response = await emailjs.send(
2659                      EMAILJS_CONFIG.serviceId,
2660                      EMAILJS_CONFIG.templateId,
2661                      templateParams
2662                  );
2663  
2664                  if (response.status === 200) {
2665                      return { success: true, method: 'EmailJS' };
2666                  } else {
2667                      throw new Error('EmailJS failed');
2668                  }
2669              } catch (error) {
2670                  console.warn('EmailJS failed:', error);
2671                  throw error;
2672              }
2673          }
2674  
2675          // Send email via mailto (fallback)
2676          function sendEmailViaMailto(formData) {
2677              const subject = encodeURIComponent(`[i9Script] ${formData.project ? 'Projeto: ' + formData.project : 'Contato do Site'}`);
2678              
2679              const body = encodeURIComponent(`
2680  ðŸš€ NOVA MENSAGEM DO SITE i9Script
2681  
2682  ðŸ‘¤ DADOS DO CLIENTE:
2683  Nome: ${formData.name}
2684  Email: ${formData.email}
2685  Tipo de Projeto: ${formData.project || 'NÃ£o especificado'}
2686  
2687  ðŸ’¬ MENSAGEM:
2688  ${formData.message}
2689  
2690  ðŸ“… Data/Hora: ${new Date().toLocaleString('pt-BR')}
2691  ðŸŒ Navegador: ${navigator.userAgent.substring(0, 100)}
2692  
2693  ---
2694  Enviado automaticamente pelo formulÃ¡rio de contato do site i9Script.
2695              `.trim());
2696  
2697              const mailtoLink = `mailto:contato@i9script.com?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
2698              
2699              // Open mailto link
2700              window.open(mailtoLink, '_blank');
2701              
2702              return { success: true, method: 'mailto' };
2703          }

2705          // Main email sending function
2706          async function sendContactEmail(formData) {
2707              try {
2708                  // Try EmailJS first (more professional)
2709                  return await sendEmailViaEmailJS(formData);
2710              } catch (emailjsError) {
2711                  console.log('EmailJS failed, using mailto fallback');
2712                  // Fallback to mailto
2713                  return sendEmailViaMailto(formData);
2714              }
2715          }

2717          // MOBILE MENU MANAGEMENT - CORRIGIDO E MELHORADO
2718          const mobileMenuToggle = document.getElementById('mobileMenuToggle');
2719          const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');
2720          const menuIcon = mobileMenuToggle.querySelector('i');
2721  
2722          function toggleMenu() {
2723              const isOpen = navLinks.classList.contains('mobile-open');
2724              
2725              if (isOpen) {
2726                  closeMenu();
2727              } else {
2728                  openMenu();
2729              }
2730          }
2731  
2732          function openMenu() {
2733              navLinks.classList.add('mobile-open');
2734              mobileMenuToggle.classList.add('active');
2735              menuIcon.className = 'fas fa-times';
2736              document.body.classList.add('menu-open');
2737              
2738              // Prevent scroll on body
2739              document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
2740              document.body.style.position = 'fixed';
2741              document.body.style.width = '100%';
2742              
2743              // Focus management for accessibility
2744              setTimeout(() => {
2745                  const firstLink = navLinks.querySelector('a');
2746                  if (firstLink) firstLink.focus();
2747              }, 100);
2748          }
2749  
2750          function closeMenu() {
2751              navLinks.classList.remove('mobile-open');
2752              mobileMenuToggle.classList.remove('active');
2753              menuIcon.className = 'fas fa-bars';
2754              document.body.classList.remove('menu-open');
2755              
2756              // Restore scroll on body
2757              document.body.style.overflow = '';
2758              document.body.style.position = '';
2759              document.body.style.width = '';
2760          }
2761  
2762          // Event listeners for mobile menu
2763          mobileMenuToggle.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
2764              e.preventDefault();
2765              e.stopPropagation();
2766              toggleMenu();
2767          });
2768  
2769          // Close menu when clicking outside (only on mobile menu links area)
2770          document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
2771              if (navLinks.classList.contains('mobile-open')) {
2772                  // Check if click is on menu content or navigation items
2773                  const isMenuContent = navLinks.contains(e.target);
2774                  const isToggleButton = mobileMenuToggle.contains(e.target);
2775                  const isThemeToggle = document.getElementById('themeToggle').contains(e.target);
2776                  const isLanguageToggle = document.getElementById('languageToggle').contains(e.target);
2777                  
2778                  if (!isMenuContent && !isToggleButton && !isThemeToggle && !isLanguageToggle) {
2779                      closeMenu();
2780                  }
2781              }
2782          });
2783  
2784          // Close menu on window resize to desktop size
2785          window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
2786              if (window.innerWidth > 768 && navLinks.classList.contains('mobile-open')) {
2787                  closeMenu();
2788              }
2789          });
2790  
2791          // Handle escape key to close menu
2792          document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
2793              if (e.key === 'Escape' && navLinks.classList.contains('mobile-open')) {
2794                  closeMenu();
2795                  mobileMenuToggle.focus();
2796              }
2797          });

2835          function validateEmail(email) {
2836              const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
2837              return emailRegex.test(email);
2838          }
2839  
2840          function validateForm(formData) {
2841              const errors = [];
2842              
2843              if (!formData.name || formData.name.trim().length < 2) {
2844                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Nome deve ter pelo menos 2 caracteres' : 'Name must have at least 2 characters');
2845              }
2846              
2847              if (!validateEmail(formData.email)) {
2848                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Email invÃ¡lido' : 'Invalid email');
2849              }
2850              
2851              if (!formData.project) {
2852                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Selecione um tipo de projeto' : 'Select a project type');
2853              }
2854              
2855              if (!formData.message || formData.message.trim().length < 10) {
2856                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Mensagem deve ter pelo menos 10 caracteres' : 'Message must have at least 10 characters');
2857              }
2858              
2859              return errors;
2860          }
2861  
2862          function preventXSS() {
2863              // Remove any potential script injections from URL parameters
2864              const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
2865              urlParams.forEach((value, key) => {
2866                  if (/<script|javascript:|data:/i.test(value)) {
2867                      console.warn('Potential XSS attempt detected and blocked');
2868                      urlParams.delete(key);
2869                  }
2870              });
2871          }
2872  
2873          // Rate limiting for form submissions
2874          let lastSubmission = 0;
2875          const SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN = 30000; // 30 seconds
2876  
2877          function checkRateLimit() {
2878              const now = Date.now();
2879              if (now - lastSubmission < SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN) {
2880                  const remainingTime = Math.ceil((SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN - (now - lastSubmission)) / 1000);
2881                  const message = currentLanguage === 'pt' 
2882                      ? `Aguarde ${remainingTime} segundos antes de enviar novamente`
2883                      : `Wait ${remainingTime} seconds before sending again`;
2884                  throw new Error(message);
2885              }
2886              lastSubmission = now;
2887          }
2888  
2889          // Content Security Policy violation reporting
2890          window.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', (e) => {
2891              console.warn('CSP Violation:', e.violatedDirective, e.blockedURI);
2892          });

3383          // Performance optimization - debounce scroll events
3384          function debounce(func, wait) {
3385              let timeout;
3386              return function executedFunction(...args) {
3387                  const later = () => {
3388                      clearTimeout(timeout);
3389                      func(...args);
3390                  };
3391                  clearTimeout(timeout);
3392                  timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
3393              };
3394          }

3397          window.addEventListener('scroll', debounce(() => {
3398              toggleBackToTop();
3399          }, 10));

1415      <script>
1416          // Cookie Management System
1417          const COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY = 'i9script_cookie_consent';
1418          const COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY = 'i9script_cookie_preferences';
1419  
1420          // Cookie utility functions
1421          function setCookie(name, value, days = 365) {
1422              const date = new Date();
1423              date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
1424              const expires = `expires=${date.toUTCString()}`;
1425              document.cookie = `${name}=${value}; ${expires}; path=/; SameSite=Strict; Secure`;
1426          }
1427  
1428          function getCookie(name) {
1429              const nameEQ = name + "=";
1430              const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
1431              for(let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
1432                  let c = ca[i];
1433                  while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
1434                  if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
1435              }
1436              return null;
1437          }
1438  
1439          function deleteCookie(name) {
1440              document.cookie = `${name}=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC; path=/;`;
1441          }
1442  
1443          // Initialize cookie system
1444          function initCookieSystem() {
1445              const consent = getCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY);
1446              
1447              if (!consent) {
1448                  // Show banner after a small delay
1449                  setTimeout(() => {
1450                      document.getElementById('cookieBanner').classList.add('show');
1451                  }, 1000);
1452              } else {
1453                  // Load saved preferences
1454                  loadCookiePreferences();
1455              }
1456          }
1457  
1458          // Accept all cookies
1459          function acceptAllCookies() {
1460              const preferences = {
1461                  essential: true,
1462                  performance: true,
1463                  marketing: true,
1464                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
1465              };
1466              
1467              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'accepted');
1468              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
1469              
1470              loadCookiePreferences();
1471              hideCookieBanner();
1472              closeCookieModal();
1473              
1474              // Initialize tracking scripts if accepted
1475              initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
1476              
1477              showNotification('Cookies aceitos com sucesso!', 'success');
1478          }
1479  
1480          // Decline optional cookies
1481          function declineOptionalCookies() {
1482              const preferences = {
1483                  essential: true,
1484                  performance: false,
1485                  marketing: false,
1486                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
1487              };
1488              
1489              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'declined');
1490              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
1491              
1492              loadCookiePreferences();
1493              hideCookieBanner();
1494              
1495              // Remove optional cookies
1496              removeOptionalCookies();
1497              
1498              showNotification('PreferÃªncias salvas. Apenas cookies essenciais serÃ£o utilizados.', 'info');
1499          }
1500  
1501          // Save custom cookie settings
1502          function saveCustomCookieSettings() {
1503              const preferences = {
1504                  essential: true, // Always true
1505                  performance: document.getElementById('performanceCookies').checked,
1506                  marketing: document.getElementById('marketingCookies').checked,
1507                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
1508              };
1509              
1510              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'custom');
1511              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
1512              
1513              loadCookiePreferences();
1514              hideCookieBanner();
1515              closeCookieModal();
1516              
1517              // Initialize only allowed tracking scripts
1518              initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
1519              
1520              // Remove disallowed cookies
1521              if (!preferences.performance || !preferences.marketing) {
1522                  removeOptionalCookies();
1523              }
1524              
1525              showNotification('Suas preferÃªncias foram salvas com sucesso!', 'success');
1526          }
1527  
1528          // Load cookie preferences
1529          function loadCookiePreferences() {
1530              const preferencesStr = getCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY);
1531              if (preferencesStr) {
1532                  try {
1533                      const preferences = JSON.parse(preferencesStr);
1534                      
1535                      // Update UI if modal is open
1536                      const performanceCheckbox = document.getElementById('performanceCookies');
1537                      const marketingCheckbox = document.getElementById('marketingCookies');
1538                      
1539                      if (performanceCheckbox) performanceCheckbox.checked = preferences.performance;
1540                      if (marketingCheckbox) marketingCheckbox.checked = preferences.marketing;
1541                      
1542                      // Initialize tracking based on preferences
1543                      initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
1544                      
1545                      return preferences;
1546                  } catch (e) {
1547                      console.warn('Error parsing cookie preferences:', e);
1548                  }
1549              }
1550              return null;
1551          }
1552  
1553          // Initialize tracking scripts based on preferences
1554          function initializeTrackingScripts(preferences) {
1555              // Only initialize if user has consented
1556              if (preferences.performance) {
1557                  // Initialize performance tracking (e.g., Google Analytics)
1558                  console.log('Performance tracking initialized');
1559                  // Example: gtag('config', 'GA_MEASUREMENT_ID');
1560              }
1561              
1562              if (preferences.marketing) {
1563                  // Initialize marketing tracking (e.g., Facebook Pixel, Google Ads)
1564                  console.log('Marketing tracking initialized');
1565                  // Example: fbq('init', 'PIXEL_ID');
1566              }
1567          }
1568  
1569          // Remove optional cookies
1570          function removeOptionalCookies() {
1571              // List of optional cookie names to remove
1572              const optionalCookies = [
1573                  '_ga', '_gid', '_gat', // Google Analytics
1574                  '_fbp', '_fbc', // Facebook
1575                  '__utm', // UTM tracking
1576                  // Add other optional cookie names here
1577              ];
1578              
1579              optionalCookies.forEach(cookieName => {
1580                  deleteCookie(cookieName);
1581              });
1582              
1583              // Clear localStorage items if needed
1584              const optionalLocalStorage = [
1585                  'analytics_data',
1586                  'marketing_data'
1587                  // Add other localStorage keys here
1588              ];
1589              
1590              optionalLocalStorage.forEach(key => {
1591                  localStorage.removeItem(key);
1592              });
1593          }

1598          }

1618          // Notification system
1619          function showNotification(message, type = 'info') {
1620              // Create notification element
1621              const notification = document.createElement('div');
1622              notification.style.cssText = `
1623                  position: fixed;
1624                  top: 20px;
1625                  right: 20px;
1626                  background: ${type === 'success' ? 'var(--cyan)' : 'var(--gold)'};
1627                  color: var(--white);
1628                  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
1629                  border-radius: 10px;
1630                  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
1631                  z-index: 10001;
1632                  font-weight: 600;
1633                  transform: translateX(100%);
1634                  transition: transform 0.3s ease;
1635                  max-width: 300px;
1636              `;
1637              notification.innerHTML = `
1638                  <i class="bi bi-${type === 'success' ? 'check-circle' : 'info-circle'}"></i>
1639                  ${message}
1640              `;
1641              
1642              document.body.appendChild(notification);
1643              
1644              // Show notification
1645              setTimeout(() => {
1646                  notification.style.transform = 'translateX(0)';
1647              }, 100);
1648              
1649              // Hide notification after 4 seconds
1650              setTimeout(() => {
1651                  notification.style.transform = 'translateX(100%)';
1652                  setTimeout(() => {
1653                      document.body.removeChild(notification);
1654                  }, 300);
1655              }, 4000);
1656          }

1658          // Theme Toggle
1659          function toggleTheme() {
1660              const body = document.body;
1661              const themeToggle = document.querySelector('.theme-toggle');
1662              const currentTheme = body.getAttribute('data-theme');
1663              const newTheme = currentTheme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
1664              
1665              // Add rotation effect
1666              themeToggle.classList.add('rotating');
1667              setTimeout(() => themeToggle.classList.remove('rotating'), 300);
1668              
1669              body.setAttribute('data-theme', newTheme);
1670              
1671              // Store theme preference (essential cookie)
1672              setCookie('i9script_theme', newTheme, 365);
1673              
1674              // Update icon
1675              const icon = themeToggle.querySelector('.theme-icon');
1676              icon.className = newTheme === 'dark' ? 'bi bi-sun-fill theme-icon' : 'bi bi-moon-fill theme-icon';
1677          }

1683          }

1703                  if (target) {
1704                      target.scrollIntoView({
1705                          behavior: 'smooth',
1706                          block: 'start'
1707                      });

1714              const header = document.querySelector('header');
1715              if (window.scrollY > 100) {
1716                  header.style.background = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.95)';
1717                  header.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(20px)';
1718              } else {
1719                  header.style.background = 'var(--bg-primary)';
1720                  header.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(10px)';
1721              }

1749              const mobileMenu = document.getElementById('mobileMenu');
1750              const menuToggle = document.querySelector('.mobile-menu-toggle');

1763              if (!mobileMenu.contains(e.target) && !menuToggle.contains(e.target)) {
1764                  mobileMenu.classList.remove('show');
1765              }
1766          });

 238      <script>
 239          let currentGradient = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #00f5ff, #FFD700)';
 240  
 241          // Elementos DOM
 242          const logoText = document.getElementById('logoText');
 243          const logoInput = document.getElementById('logoInput');
 244          const fontSizeSlider = document.getElementById('fontSizeSlider');
 245          const fontSizeValue = document.getElementById('fontSizeValue');
 246          const fontWeightSelect = document.getElementById('fontWeight');
 247          const gradientPreviews = document.querySelectorAll('.gradient-preview');
 248  
 249          // Event Listeners
 250          logoInput.addEventListener('input', updateLogoText);
 251          fontSizeSlider.addEventListener('input', updateFontSize);
 252          fontWeightSelect.addEventListener('change', updateFontWeight);
 253  
 254          gradientPreviews.forEach(preview => {
 255              preview.addEventListener('click', () => {
 256                  gradientPreviews.forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
 257                  preview.classList.add('active');
 258                  currentGradient = preview.dataset.gradient;
 259                  updateGradient();
 260              });
 261          });
 262  
 263          function updateLogoText() {
 264              logoText.textContent = logoInput.value || 'i9Script';
 265          }
 266  
 267          function updateFontSize() {
 268              const size = fontSizeSlider.value;
 269              logoText.style.fontSize = size + 'rem';
 270              fontSizeValue.textContent = size + 'rem';
 271          }
 272  
 273          function updateFontWeight() {
 274              logoText.style.fontWeight = fontWeightSelect.value;
 275          }
 276  
 277          function updateGradient() {
 278              logoText.style.background = currentGradient;
 279              logoText.style.webkitBackgroundClip = 'text';
 280              logoText.style.backgroundClip = 'text';
 281          }
 282  
 283          function downloadLogo(width, height, filename) {
 284              const canvas = document.getElementById('logoCanvas');
 285              const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
 286              
 287              // Configurar canvas
 288              canvas.width = width;
 289              canvas.height = height;
 290              
 291              // Configurar proporÃ§Ãµes
 292              const fontSize = Math.min(width, height) * 0.15;
 293              const text = logoInput.value || 'i9Script';
 294              
 295              // Fundo transparente
 296              ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
 297              
 298              // Configurar fonte
 299              ctx.font = `${fontWeightSelect.value} ${fontSize}px Orbitron, sans-serif`;
 300              ctx.textAlign = 'center';
 301              ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
 302              
 303              // Criar gradiente
 304              const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
 305              
 306              // Extrair cores do gradiente atual
 307              if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#FFD700')) {
 308                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
 309                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FFD700');
 310              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#2563eb')) {
 311                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
 312                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#2563eb');
 313              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#FFD700') && currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b')) {
 314                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#FFD700');
 315                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ff6b6b');
 316              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b') && currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff')) {
 317                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#ff6b6b');
 318                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#00f5ff');
 319              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#FFD700') && currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b')) {
 320                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
 321                  gradient.addColorStop(0.5, '#FFD700');
 322                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ff6b6b');
 323              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#8b5cf6') && currentGradient.includes('#06b6d4')) {
 324                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#8b5cf6');
 325                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#06b6d4');
 326              } else {
 327                  // Fallback
 328                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
 329                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FFD700');
 330              }
 331              
 332              ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
 333              ctx.fillText(text, width / 2, height / 2);
 334              
 335              // Download
 336              const link = document.createElement('a');
 337              link.download = filename + '.png';
 338              link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
 339              link.click();
 340              
 341              // Feedback visual
 342              const button = event.target;
 343              const originalText = button.textContent;
 344              button.textContent = 'âœ“ Baixado!';
 345              button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #22c55e, #16a34a)';
 346              
 347              setTimeout(() => {
 348                  button.textContent = originalText;
 349                  button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #00f5ff, #2563eb)';
 350              }, 2000);
 351          }
 352  
 353          function downloadAllSizes() {
 354              const sizes = [
 355                  { width: 32, height: 32, name: 'i9script-favicon' },
 356                  { width: 64, height: 64, name: 'i9script-icon-small' },
 357                  { width: 128, height: 128, name: 'i9script-icon-medium' },
 358                  { width: 256, height: 256, name: 'i9script-logo' },
 359                  { width: 512, height: 512, name: 'i9script-logo-hd' },
 360                  { width: 1024, height: 1024, name: 'i9script-logo-4k' }
 361              ];
 362              
 363              const button = event.target;
 364              const originalText = button.textContent;
 365              let downloadCount = 0;
 366              
 367              button.textContent = 'â³ Gerando...';
 368              button.disabled = true;
 369              
 370              sizes.forEach((size, index) => {
 371                  setTimeout(() => {
 372                      downloadLogo(size.width, size.height, size.name);
 373                      downloadCount++;
 374                      
 375                      button.textContent = `ðŸ“¦ ${downloadCount}/${sizes.length} concluÃ­do`;
 376                      
 377                      if (downloadCount === sizes.length) {
 378                          button.textContent = 'âœ… Todos baixados!';
 379                          button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #22c55e, #16a34a)';
 380                          
 381                          setTimeout(() => {
 382                              button.textContent = originalText;
 383                              button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #00f5ff, #2563eb)';
 384                              button.disabled = false;
 385                          }, 3000);
 386                      }
 387                  }, index * 500);
 388              });
 389          }
 390  
 391          // Carregar fonte e inicializar
 392          document.fonts.ready.then(() => {
 393              console.log('ðŸŽ¨ Fonte Orbitron carregada com sucesso!');
 394              console.log('âœ¨ Gerador de Logo i9Script pronto para uso!');
 395          });
 396      </script>
 397  </body>
 398  </html>

  16          expires 1y;
  17          add_header Cache-Control "public, immutable";

  51      }

2942  2615          // Initialize EmailJS
2943  2616          function initEmailJS() {
2944  2617              if (typeof emailjs !== 'undefined') {
2945  2618                  try {
2946  2619                      emailjs.init(EMAILJS_CONFIG.publicKey);
2947  2620                      return true;
2948  2621                  } catch (error) {
2949  2622                      console.warn('EmailJS initialization failed:', error);
2950  2623                      return false;
2951  2624                  }
2952  2625              }
2953  2626              return false;
2954  2627          }
2955  2628  
2956  2629          // Check if EmailJS is properly configured
2957  2630          function isEmailJSConfigured() {
2958  2631              return EMAILJS_CONFIG.publicKey !== 'YOUR_PUBLIC_KEY' && 
2959  2632                     EMAILJS_CONFIG.serviceId !== 'YOUR_SERVICE_ID' && 
2960  2633                     EMAILJS_CONFIG.templateId !== 'YOUR_TEMPLATE_ID';
2961  2634          }
2962  2635  
2963  2636          // Send email via EmailJS
2964  2637          async function sendEmailViaEmailJS(formData) {
2965  2638              try {
2966  2639                  if (!isEmailJSConfigured()) {
2967  2640                      throw new Error('EmailJS not configured - using fallback');
2968  2641                  }
2969  2642  
2970  2643                  if (!initEmailJS()) {
2971  2644                      throw new Error('EmailJS not available');
2972  2645                  }
2973  2646  
2974  2647                  const templateParams = {
2975  2648                      from_name: formData.name,
2976  2649                      from_email: formData.email,
2977  2650                      project_type: formData.project,
2978  2651                      message: formData.message,
2979  2652                      to_email: 'contato@i9script.com',
2980  2653                      reply_to: formData.email,
2981  2654                      date: new Date().toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
2982  2655                      user_agent: navigator.userAgent.substring(0, 100)
2983  2656                  };
2984  2657  
2985  2658                  const response = await emailjs.send(
2986  2659                      EMAILJS_CONFIG.serviceId,
2987  2660                      EMAILJS_CONFIG.templateId,
2988  2661                      templateParams
2989  2662                  );
2990  2663  
2991  2664                  if (response.status === 200) {
2992  2665                      return { success: true, method: 'EmailJS' };
2993  2666                  } else {
2994  2667                      throw new Error('EmailJS failed');
2995  2668                  }
2996  2669              } catch (error) {
2997  2670                  console.warn('EmailJS failed:', error);
2998  2671                  throw error;
2999  2672              }
3000  2673          }
3001  2674  
3002  2675          // Send email via mailto (fallback)
3003  2676          function sendEmailViaMailto(formData) {
3004  2677              const subject = encodeURIComponent(`[i9Script] ${formData.project ? 'Projeto: ' + formData.project : 'Contato do Site'}`);
3005  2678              
3006  2679              const body = encodeURIComponent(`
3007  2680  ðŸš€ NOVA MENSAGEM DO SITE i9Script
3008  2681  
3009  2682  ðŸ‘¤ DADOS DO CLIENTE:
3010  2683  Nome: ${formData.name}
3011  2684  Email: ${formData.email}
3012  2685  Tipo de Projeto: ${formData.project || 'NÃ£o especificado'}
3013  2686  
3014  2687  ðŸ’¬ MENSAGEM:
3015  2688  ${formData.message}
3016  2689  
3017  2690  ðŸ“… Data/Hora: ${new Date().toLocaleString('pt-BR')}
3018  2691  ðŸŒ Navegador: ${navigator.userAgent.substring(0, 100)}
3019  2692  
3020  2693  ---
3021  2694  Enviado automaticamente pelo formulÃ¡rio de contato do site i9Script.
3022  2695              `.trim());
3023  2696  
3024  2697              const mailtoLink = `mailto:contato@i9script.com?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
3025  2698              
3026  2699              // Open mailto link
3027  2700              window.open(mailtoLink, '_blank');
3028  2701              
3029  2702              return { success: true, method: 'mailto' };
3030  2703          }

3032  2705          // Main email sending function
3033  2706          async function sendContactEmail(formData) {
3034  2707              try {
3035  2708                  // Try EmailJS first (more professional)
3036  2709                  return await sendEmailViaEmailJS(formData);
3037  2710              } catch (emailjsError) {
3038  2711                  console.log('EmailJS failed, using mailto fallback');
3039  2712                  // Fallback to mailto
3040  2713                  return sendEmailViaMailto(formData);
3041  2714              }
3042  2715          }

3167  2840          function validateForm(formData) {
3168  2841              const errors = [];
3169  2842              
3170  2843              if (!formData.name || formData.name.trim().length < 2) {
3171  2844                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Nome deve ter pelo menos 2 caracteres' : 'Name must have at least 2 characters');
3172  2845              }
3173  2846              
3174  2847              if (!validateEmail(formData.email)) {
3175  2848                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Email invÃ¡lido' : 'Invalid email');
3176  2849              }
3177  2850              
3178  2851              if (!formData.project) {
3179  2852                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Selecione um tipo de projeto' : 'Select a project type');
3180  2853              }
3181  2854              
3182  2855              if (!formData.message || formData.message.trim().length < 10) {
3183  2856                  errors.push(currentLanguage === 'pt' ? 'Mensagem deve ter pelo menos 10 caracteres' : 'Message must have at least 10 characters');
3184  2857              }
3185  2858              
3186  2859              return errors;
3187  2860          }

3189  2862          function preventXSS() {
3190  2863              // Remove any potential script injections from URL parameters
3191  2864              const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
3192  2865              urlParams.forEach((value, key) => {
3193  2866                  if (/<script|javascript:|data:/i.test(value)) {
3194  2867                      console.warn('Potential XSS attempt detected and blocked');
3195  2868                      urlParams.delete(key);
3196  2869                  }
3197  2870              });
3198  2871          }

3200  2873          // Rate limiting for form submissions
3201  2874          let lastSubmission = 0;
3202  2875          const SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN = 30000; // 30 seconds
3203  2876  
3204  2877          function checkRateLimit() {
3205  2878              const now = Date.now();
3206  2879              if (now - lastSubmission < SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN) {
3207  2880                  const remainingTime = Math.ceil((SUBMISSION_COOLDOWN - (now - lastSubmission)) / 1000);
3208  2881                  const message = currentLanguage === 'pt' 
3209  2882                      ? `Aguarde ${remainingTime} segundos antes de enviar novamente`
3210  2883                      : `Wait ${remainingTime} seconds before sending again`;
3211  2884                  throw new Error(message);
3212  2885              }
3213  2886              lastSubmission = now;
3214  2887          }

3671  3344                  e.preventDefault();
3672  3345                  const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
3673  3346                  
3674  3347                  // Validate target ID to prevent XSS
3675  3348                  if (!/^#[a-zA-Z][\w-]*$/.test(targetId)) {

3677  3350                      return;

3681  3354                  if (targetElement) {
3682  3355                      // Close mobile menu if open
3683  3356                      closeMenu();
3684  3357                      
3685  3358                      // Smooth scroll to target

3710  3383          // Performance optimization - debounce scroll events
3711  3384          function debounce(func, wait) {
3712  3385              let timeout;
3713  3386              return function executedFunction(...args) {
3714  3387                  const later = () => {
3715  3388                      clearTimeout(timeout);
3716  3389                      func(...args);
3717  3390                  };
3718  3391                  clearTimeout(timeout);
3719  3392                  timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
3720  3393              };
3721  3394          }
3722  3395  
3723  3396          // Apply debounce to scroll events
3724  3397          window.addEventListener('scroll', debounce(() => {
3725  3398              toggleBackToTop();
3726  3399          }, 10));

3740  3413              }
3741  3414          }

5173  1416          // Cookie Management System
5174  1417          const COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY = 'i9script_cookie_consent';
5175  1418          const COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY = 'i9script_cookie_preferences';
5176  1419  
5177  1420          // Cookie utility functions
5178  1421          function setCookie(name, value, days = 365) {
5179  1422              const date = new Date();
5180  1423              date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
5181  1424              const expires = `expires=${date.toUTCString()}`;
5182  1425              document.cookie = `${name}=${value}; ${expires}; path=/; SameSite=Strict; Secure`;
5183  1426          }
5184  1427  
5185  1428          function getCookie(name) {
5186  1429              const nameEQ = name + "=";
5187  1430              const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
5188  1431              for(let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
5189  1432                  let c = ca[i];
5190  1433                  while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
5191  1434                  if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
5192  1435              }
5193  1436              return null;
5194  1437          }
5195  1438  
5196  1439          function deleteCookie(name) {
5197  1440              document.cookie = `${name}=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC; path=/;`;
5198  1441          }
5199  1442  
5200  1443          // Initialize cookie system
5201  1444          function initCookieSystem() {
5202  1445              const consent = getCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY);
5203  1446              
5204  1447              if (!consent) {
5205  1448                  // Show banner after a small delay
5206  1449                  setTimeout(() => {
5207  1450                      document.getElementById('cookieBanner').classList.add('show');
5208  1451                  }, 1000);
5209  1452              } else {
5210  1453                  // Load saved preferences
5211  1454                  loadCookiePreferences();
5212  1455              }
5213  1456          }
5214  1457  
5215  1458          // Accept all cookies
5216  1459          function acceptAllCookies() {
5217  1460              const preferences = {
5218  1461                  essential: true,
5219  1462                  performance: true,
5220  1463                  marketing: true,
5221  1464                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
5222  1465              };
5223  1466              
5224  1467              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'accepted');
5225  1468              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
5226  1469              
5227  1470              loadCookiePreferences();
5228  1471              hideCookieBanner();
5229  1472              closeCookieModal();
5230  1473              
5231  1474              // Initialize tracking scripts if accepted
5232  1475              initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
5233  1476              
5234  1477              showNotification('Cookies aceitos com sucesso!', 'success');
5235  1478          }
5236  1479  
5237  1480          // Decline optional cookies

5240  1483                  essential: true,
5241  1484                  performance: false,
5242  1485                  marketing: false,
5243  1486                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
5244  1487              };
5245  1488              
5246  1489              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'declined');
5247  1490              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
5248  1491

5251  1494

5253  1496              removeOptionalCookies();

5256  1499          }

5258  1501          // Save custom cookie settings
5259  1502          function saveCustomCookieSettings() {
5260  1503              const preferences = {
5261  1504                  essential: true, // Always true
5262  1505                  performance: document.getElementById('performanceCookies').checked,
5263  1506                  marketing: document.getElementById('marketingCookies').checked,
5264  1507                  timestamp: new Date().getTime()
5265  1508              };
5266  1509              
5267  1510              setCookie(COOKIE_CONSENT_KEY, 'custom');
5268  1511              setCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY, JSON.stringify(preferences));
5269  1512              
5270  1513              loadCookiePreferences();
5271  1514              hideCookieBanner();
5272  1515              closeCookieModal();
5273  1516              
5274  1517              // Initialize only allowed tracking scripts
5275  1518              initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
5276  1519              
5277  1520              // Remove disallowed cookies
5278  1521              if (!preferences.performance || !preferences.marketing) {
5279  1522                  removeOptionalCookies();
5280  1523              }
5281  1524              
5282  1525              showNotification('Suas preferÃªncias foram salvas com sucesso!', 'success');
5283  1526          }
5284  1527  
5285  1528          // Load cookie preferences
5286  1529          function loadCookiePreferences() {
5287  1530              const preferencesStr = getCookie(COOKIE_PREFERENCES_KEY);
5288  1531              if (preferencesStr) {
5289  1532                  try {
5290  1533                      const preferences = JSON.parse(preferencesStr);
5291  1534                      
5292  1535                      // Update UI if modal is open
5293  1536                      const performanceCheckbox = document.getElementById('performanceCookies');
5294  1537                      const marketingCheckbox = document.getElementById('marketingCookies');
5295  1538                      
5296  1539                      if (performanceCheckbox) performanceCheckbox.checked = preferences.performance;
5297  1540                      if (marketingCheckbox) marketingCheckbox.checked = preferences.marketing;
5298  1541                      
5299  1542                      // Initialize tracking based on preferences
5300  1543                      initializeTrackingScripts(preferences);
5301  1544                      
5302  1545                      return preferences;
5303  1546                  } catch (e) {
5304  1547                      console.warn('Error parsing cookie preferences:', e);
5305  1548                  }
5306  1549              }
5307  1550              return null;
5308  1551          }
5309  1552  
5310  1553          // Initialize tracking scripts based on preferences
5311  1554          function initializeTrackingScripts(preferences) {
5312  1555              // Only initialize if user has consented
5313  1556              if (preferences.performance) {
5314  1557                  // Initialize performance tracking (e.g., Google Analytics)
5315  1558                  console.log('Performance tracking initialized');
5316  1559                  // Example: gtag('config', 'GA_MEASUREMENT_ID');
5317  1560              }
5318  1561              
5319  1562              if (preferences.marketing) {
5320  1563                  // Initialize marketing tracking (e.g., Facebook Pixel, Google Ads)
5321  1564                  console.log('Marketing tracking initialized');
5322  1565                  // Example: fbq('init', 'PIXEL_ID');
5323  1566              }
5324  1567          }
5325  1568  
5326  1569          // Remove optional cookies
5327  1570          function removeOptionalCookies() {
5328  1571              // List of optional cookie names to remove
5329  1572              const optionalCookies = [
5330  1573                  '_ga', '_gid', '_gat', // Google Analytics
5331  1574                  '_fbp', '_fbc', // Facebook
5332  1575                  '__utm', // UTM tracking
5333  1576                  // Add other optional cookie names here
5334  1577              ];
5335  1578              
5336  1579              optionalCookies.forEach(cookieName => {
5337  1580                  deleteCookie(cookieName);
5338  1581              });
5339  1582              
5340  1583              // Clear localStorage items if needed
5341  1584              const optionalLocalStorage = [
5342  1585                  'analytics_data',
5343  1586                  'marketing_data'
5344  1587                  // Add other localStorage keys here
5345  1588              ];
5346  1589              
5347  1590              optionalLocalStorage.forEach(key => {
5348  1591                  localStorage.removeItem(key);
5349  1592              });
5350  1593          }

5871   258                  currentGradient = preview.dataset.gradient;
5872   259                  updateGradient();
5873   260              });

5896   283          function downloadLogo(width, height, filename) {
5897   284              const canvas = document.getElementById('logoCanvas');
5898   285              const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
5899   286              
5900   287              // Configurar canvas
5901   288              canvas.width = width;
5902   289              canvas.height = height;
5903   290              
5904   291              // Configurar proporÃ§Ãµes
5905   292              const fontSize = Math.min(width, height) * 0.15;
5906   293              const text = logoInput.value || 'i9Script';
5907   294              
5908   295              // Fundo transparente
5909   296              ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
5910   297              
5911   298              // Configurar fonte
5912   299              ctx.font = `${fontWeightSelect.value} ${fontSize}px Orbitron, sans-serif`;
5913   300              ctx.textAlign = 'center';
5914   301              ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
5915   302              
5916   303              // Criar gradiente
5917   304              const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, height);
5918   305              
5919   306              // Extrair cores do gradiente atual
5920   307              if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#FFD700')) {
5921   308                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
5922   309                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FFD700');
5923   310              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#2563eb')) {
5924   311                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
5925   312                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#2563eb');
5926   313              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#FFD700') && currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b')) {
5927   314                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#FFD700');
5928   315                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ff6b6b');
5929   316              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b') && currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff')) {
5930   317                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#ff6b6b');
5931   318                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#00f5ff');
5932   319              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#00f5ff') && currentGradient.includes('#FFD700') && currentGradient.includes('#ff6b6b')) {
5933   320                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
5934   321                  gradient.addColorStop(0.5, '#FFD700');
5935   322                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ff6b6b');
5936   323              } else if (currentGradient.includes('#8b5cf6') && currentGradient.includes('#06b6d4')) {
5937   324                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#8b5cf6');
5938   325                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#06b6d4');
5939   326              } else {
5940   327                  // Fallback
5941   328                  gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00f5ff');
5942   329                  gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FFD700');
5943   330              }
5944   331              
5945   332              ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
5946   333              ctx.fillText(text, width / 2, height / 2);
5947   334              
5948   335              // Download
5949   336              const link = document.createElement('a');
5950   337              link.download = filename + '.png';
5951   338              link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
5952   339              link.click();
5953   340              
5954   341              // Feedback visual
5955   342              const button = event.target;
5956   343              const originalText = button.textContent;
5957   344              button.textContent = 'âœ“ Baixado!';
5958   345              button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #22c55e, #16a34a)';
5959   346              
5960   347              setTimeout(() => {
5961   348                  button.textContent = originalText;
5962   349                  button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #00f5ff, #2563eb)';
5963   350              }, 2000);
5964   351          }

5966   353          function downloadAllSizes() {
5967   354              const sizes = [
5968   355                  { width: 32, height: 32, name: 'i9script-favicon' },
5969   356                  { width: 64, height: 64, name: 'i9script-icon-small' },
5970   357                  { width: 128, height: 128, name: 'i9script-icon-medium' },
5971   358                  { width: 256, height: 256, name: 'i9script-logo' },
5972   359                  { width: 512, height: 512, name: 'i9script-logo-hd' },
5973   360                  { width: 1024, height: 1024, name: 'i9script-logo-4k' }
5974   361              ];
5975   362              
5976   363              const button = event.target;
5977   364              const originalText = button.textContent;
5978   365              let downloadCount = 0;
5979   366              
5980   367              button.textContent = 'â³ Gerando...';
5981   368              button.disabled = true;
5982   369              
5983   370              sizes.forEach((size, index) => {
5984   371                  setTimeout(() => {
5985   372                      downloadLogo(size.width, size.height, size.name);
5986   373                      downloadCount++;
5987   374                      
5988   375                      button.textContent = `ðŸ“¦ ${downloadCount}/${sizes.length} concluÃ­do`;
5989   376                      
5990   377                      if (downloadCount === sizes.length) {
5991   378                          button.textContent = 'âœ… Todos baixados!';
5992   379                          button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #22c55e, #16a34a)';
5993   380                          
5994   381                          setTimeout(() => {
5995   382                              button.textContent = originalText;
5996   383                              button.style.background = 'linear-gradient(135deg, #00f5ff, #2563eb)';
5997   384                              button.disabled = false;
5998   385                          }, 3000);
5999   386                      }
6000   387                  }, index * 500);
6001   388              });
6002   389          }

6040    23      location ~* \.html$ {
6041    24          expires -1;
6042    25          add_header Cache-Control "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate";
6043    26          add_header Pragma "no-cache";
6044    27          add_header X-Frame-Options DENY always;
6045    28          add_header X-Content-Type-Options nosniff always;
6046    29      }
6047    30

6049    32      location / {
6050    33          try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
6051    34          
6052    35          # Headers de seguranÃ§a
6053    36          add_header X-Frame-Options DENY always;

6060    43          add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Origin, Content-Type, Accept, Authorization" always;
6061    44      }
6062    45  
6063    46      # Health check endpoint
6064    47      location /health {
6065    48          access_log off;
6066    49          return 200 "healthy\n";
6067    50          add_header Content-Type text/plain;
6068    51      }
6069    52  
6070    53      # Bloquear acesso a arquivos sensÃ­veis
6071    54      location ~ /\. {
6072    55          deny all;
6073    56          access_log off;
6074    57          log_not_found off;
6075    58      }
6076    59  
6077    60      location ~ \.(env|log|conf)$ {
6078    61          deny all;
6079    62          access_log off;
6080    63          log_not_found off;
6081    64      }
6082    65  
6083    66      # ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes de logging
6084    67      access_log /var/log/nginx/access.log;
6085    68      error_log /var/log/nginx/error.log warn;
6086    69  
6087    70      # ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes de compressÃ£o
6088    71      gzip on;

6090    73      gzip_min_length 1024;
6091    74      gzip_types

1974          // Debounce function for performance
1975          function debounce(func, wait) {
1976              let timeout;
1977              return function executedFunction(...args) {
1978                  const later = () => {
1979                      clearTimeout(timeout);
1980                      func(...args);
1981                  };
1982                  clearTimeout(timeout);
1983                  timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
1984              };
1985          }
1986  
1987          window.addEventListener('resize', debounce(handleResponsiveChanges, 100));

1999          function preventXSS() {
2000              // Remove any potential script injections from URL parameters
2001              const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
2002              urlParams.forEach((value, key) => {
2003                  if (/<script|javascript:|data:/i.test(value)) {
2004                      console.warn('Potential XSS attempt detected and blocked');
2005                      urlParams.delete(key);
2006                  }
2007              });
2008          }
